- **Backend live**: `nwfl-backend.duckdns.org` (Gunicorn, systemd `nwfl-api`)

</environment>

<completed_tasks>
- **Season-based navigation**: `season` CharField on Match, `?season=` filter, `/api/matches/seasons/` endpoint, season selector in admin
- **Notes field**: Added `notes` TextField, synced from sheet Notes column, editable in MatchForm
- **Pagination fix**: Removed pagination then added `MatchListSerializer` (excludes goals) to prevent gunicorn timeout on 557 matches
- **Season filter race condition**: Added `seasonsReady` flag so `loadMatches()` waits for default season before fetching
- **Multi-season backfill**: Fixed sync script to read `Season` column per row instead of defaulting to `--season` arg. DB now has 2021/22, 2023/24, 2024/25, 2025/26
- **Updated roadmap PDF**: Created via document-edit MCP with completed features marked
</completed_tasks>

<active_issues>
- **🔴 Standing model schema bug**: `Standing.team` is `OneToOneField` — prevents one team having standings in multiple seasons. Must change to `ForeignKey` with `unique_together=['team','season']`
- **🔴 Standings auto-calc**: `recalculate_standings` command exists but writes to single season rows. Needs season-aware aggregation
- **🔴 HTHG/HTAG missing**: Half-time scores not in Match model yet
- **🔴 Fixtures/schedule separation**: No distinction between upcoming fixtures and completed results in API/views
- **🔴 Dashboard match count**: `api.getMatches()` without season filter returns all 557 matches
</active_issues>

<file>
nwfl-backend/matches/views.py

**Summary:**
MatchViewSet with season filtering, lightweight list serializer, and seasons action.

**Key elements:**
- `get_queryset()` filters by `season`, `status`, `matchday`, `group`
- `pagination_class = None`
- `get_serializer_class()` returns `MatchListSerializer` for list, `MatchSerializer` for retrieve
- `@action seasons` returns distinct seasons ordered newest first

**Latest version:**
```python
class MatchViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    serializer_class = MatchSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticatedOrReadOnly]
    pagination_class = None

    def get_serializer_class(self):
        if self.action == 'list':
            return MatchListSerializer
        return MatchSerializer

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = Match.objects.select_related('home_team', 'away_team').prefetch_related('goals').all()
        season = self.request.query_params.get('season')
        status = self.request.query_params.get('status')
        matchday = self.request.query_params.get('matchday')
        group = self.request.query_params.get('group')
        if season: qs = qs.filter(season=season)
        if status: qs = qs.filter(status=status)
        if matchday: qs = qs.filter(matchday=matchday)
        if group: qs = qs.filter(home_team__group=group)
        return qs

    @action(detail=False, methods=['get'])
    def seasons(self, request):
        seasons = Match.objects.exclude(season='').values_list('season', flat=True).distinct().order_by('-season')
        return Response(list(seasons))
```
</file>

nwfl-backend/matches/serializers.py

**Summary:**
MatchSerializer with nested goals, and lightweight MatchListSerializer for list views.

**Key elements:**
- `MatchSerializer`: includes `home_team`/`away_team` TeamSerializer + `goals` GoalSerializer
- `MatchListSerializer`: same fields EXCEPT `goals` removed for performance

**Latest version:**
```python
class MatchListSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    home_team = TeamSerializer(read_only=True)
    away_team = TeamSerializer(read_only=True)
    class Meta:
        model = Match
        fields = ['id','home_team','away_team','season','matchday','date','kick_off','venue','status','home_score','away_score','pending_reason','notify_admin','notes']

class MatchSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    home_team = TeamSerializer(read_only=True)
    away_team = TeamSerializer(read_only=True)
    goals = GoalSerializer(many=True, read_only=True)
    home_team_id = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(source='home_team', queryset=Team.objects.all(), write_only=True)
    away_team_id = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(source='away_team', queryset=Team.objects.all(), write_only=True)
    class Meta:
        model = Match
        fields = [...'notes','goals']
```
</file>

<file>
nwfl-backend/matches/management/commands/sync_from_sheet.py

**Summary:**
Syncs from Google Sheet. Now reads Season column per row and Notes column.

**Key logic:**
- `_process_row()` reads `row.get('Season', season)` and `row.get('Notes', '')`
- `_parse_remarks()` maps "Completed"→FT, "Postponed"→PENDING, etc.
- Fixtures mode (`--fixtures`) sets status UPCOMING, null scores
- Regular mode reads FTHG/FTAG for scores
- After sync: calls `recalculate_standings`

**Latest version (excerpt):**
```python
sheet_season = str(row.get('Season', '')).strip()
if sheet_season:
    season = sheet_season
# ...
match.notes = str(row.get('Notes', '')).strip()
match.season = season
match.save()
```

<file>
nwfl-admin/src/pages/Matches/index.tsx

**Summary:**
Matches page with season dropdown, filters, and results/desk views.

**Key fix:**
- Added `seasonsReady` state to prevent `loadMatches()` firing before seasons load
- `loadSeasons()` sets default season then `setSeasonsReady(true)`
- `useEffect` for matches only runs when `seasonsReady` is true

**Latest version (excerpt):**
```tsx
const [season, setSeason] = useState('')
const [seasons, setSeasons] = useState<string[]>([])
const [seasonsReady, setSeasonsReady] = useState(false)

async function loadSeasons() {
  const response = await api.getSeasons()
  setSeasons(response)
  if (response.length && !season) setSeason(response[0])
  setSeasonsReady(true)
}

useEffect(() => { void loadSeasons() }, [])
useEffect(() => { if (seasonsReady) void loadMatches() }, [season, filters.matchday, filters.status, seasonsReady])
```
</file>

<important_context>
- **DB seasons distribution**: 2021/22 (72 matches), 2023/24 (112), 2024/25 (104), 2025/26 (172). Plus 97 matches with no date.
- **Gunicorn timeout fix**: Removing pagination caused 500s because serializing 557 matches with nested goals exceeded 30s worker timeout. Fixed by using `MatchListSerializer` (no goals) for list views.
- **Sheet Season column**: Previously ignored; all rows defaulted to `--season` arg. Fixed to read per-row.
- **Standing recalc command**: `recalculate_standings.py` aggregates all FT matches with non-null scores, but writes to `Standing.objects.get_or_create(team=team)` — no season filtering, and schema blocks multiple seasons.
- **Next immediate task**: Change Standing model from OneToOne to ForeignKey+unique_together(team,season), update serializer/view, make recalc command season-aware, then add HTHG/HTAG fields.
</important_context>

{

  "content": "from django.db.models.signals import post_save\nfrom django.dispatch import receiver\nfrom .models import Match\n\n\n@receiver(post_save, sender=Match)\ndef recalculate_standings_on_match_save(sender, instance, **kwargs):\n    \"\"\"Auto-recalculate standings for the match's season whenever a result is saved.\"\"\"\n    if instance.status != Match.STATUS_FT:\n        return\n    if instance.home_score is None or instance.away_score is None:\n        return\n    if not instance.season:\n        return\n\n    from django.db import transaction\n    from teams.models import Team\n    from standings.models import Standing\n\n    def _do_recalc():\n        # Aggregate stats for this season only\n        completed = Match.objects.filter(\n            season=instance.season,\n            status=Match.STATUS_FT,\n            home_score__isnull=False,\n            away_score__isnull=False,\n        ).select_related('home_team', 'away_team')\n\n        # Get teams that actually have matches this season\n        team_ids = set()\n        for m in completed:\n            team_ids.add(m.home_team_id)\n            team_ids.add(m.away_team_id)\n\n        stats = {}\n        for team in Team.objects.filter(id__in=team_ids):\n            stats[team.id] = {\n                'team': team,\n                'played': 0, 'won': 0, 'drawn': 0, 'lost': 0,\n                'gf': 0, 'ga': 0,\n            }\n\n        for m in completed:\n            hs, ag = m.home_score or 0, m.away_score or 0\n            hid, aid = m.home_team_id, m.away_team_id\n            if hid not in stats or aid not in stats:\n                continue\n            stats[hid]['played'] += 1\n            stats[hid]['gf'] += hs\n            stats[hid]['ga'] += ag\n            stats[aid]['played'] += 1\n            stats[aid]['gf'] += ag\n            stats[aid]['ga'] += hs\n            if hs > ag:\n                stats[hid]['won'] += 1\n                stats[aid]['lost'] += 1\n            elif hs < ag:\n                stats[aid]['won'] += 1\n                stats[hid]['lost'] += 1\n            else:\n                stats[hid]['drawn'] += 1\n                stats[aid]['drawn'] += 1\n\n        for team_id, s in stats.items():\n            pts = s['won'] * 3 + s['drawn']\n            standing, _ = Standing.objects.get_or_create(\n                team=s['team'], season=instance.season\n            )\n            standing.played = s['played']\n            standing.won = s['won']\n            standing.drawn = s['drawn']\n            standing.lost = s['lost']\n            standing.gf = s['gf']\n            standing.ga = s['ga']\n            standing.points = pts\n            standing.save()\n\n    transaction.on_commit(_do_recalc)\n"
}
```

self.ga\n\n    @property\n    def form(self):\n        \"\"\"Return last 5 results (W/D/L) for this team in this season.\"\"\"\n        from matches.models import Match\n        results = []\n        matches = Match.objects.filter(\n            season=self.season,\n            status=Match.STATUS_FT,\n            home_score__isnull=False,\n            away_score__isnull=False,\n        ).filter(\n            models.Q(home_team=self.team) | models.Q(away_team=self.team)\n        ).order_by('-date')[:5]\n        for m in matches:\n            if m.home_team == self.team:\n                if m.home_score > m.away_score:\n                    results.append('W')\n                elif m.home_score < m.away_score:\n                    results.append('L')\n                else:\n                    results.append('D')\n            else:\n                if m.away_score > m.home_score:\n                    results.append('W')\n                elif m.away_score < m.home_score:\n                    results.append('L')\n                else:\n                    results.append('D')\n        return results\n\n    def __str__(self):\n        return f\"{self.team.name} ({self.season})\"\n"

   120	    def _build_name_map(self, teams):
   121	        """Build fuzzy name lookup for team matching."""
   122	        mapping = {}
   123	        for team in teams:

   129	    def _find_team(self, name, name_map):
   130	        """Fuzzy-match a team name from the sheet to a DB team."""
   131	        key = name.strip().lower()
   132	        # Strip common suffixes
   133	        for suffix in [' fc', ' football club', ' ladies fc', ' ladies', ' queens fc']:
   134	            if key.endswith(suffix):
   135	                key = key[:-len(suffix)]
   136	
   137	        if key in name_map:
   138	            return name_map[key]

   142	            if key in candidate or candidate in key:
   143	                return team
   144

   151	                return datetime.strptime(str(value).strip(), fmt).date()

   168	    def _parse_remarks(self, remarks):

   177	    def _parse_goals(self, goal_str):

   182	        goals = []

   244	
   245	        action = 'updated' if match else 'created'
   246

   251	            return {'action': action, 'goals': 0}

   253	        if not match:

   267	        match.venue = venue or match.venue
   268	        match.matchday = matchday
   269	        match.notes = notes

   272	        # Parse and create goals

   275	        away_goals_str = row.get('AwayTeam Goals', '')
   276	
   277	        if home_goals_str or away_goals_str:
   278	            # Clear existing goals for this match to avoid duplicates
   279	            match.goals.all().delete()

   305	    def _process_fixture_row(self, row, season, name_map, dry_run):
   306	        """Process a fixtures sheet row (no scores, status=UPCOMING)."""
   307	        home_name = str(row.get('Home Team', '')).strip()
   308	        away_name = str(row.get('Away Team', '')).strip()
   309	
   310	        if not home_name or not away_name:
   311	            return None
   312	
   313	        home_team = self._find_team(home_name, name_map)
   314	        away_team = self._find_team(away_name, name_map)
   315	
   316	        if not home_team or not away_team:
   317	            self.stdout.write(self.style.WARNING(
   318	                f'Skipping fixture row: unknown team(s) "{home_name}" vs "{away_name}"'
   319	            ))
   320	            return None
   321	
   322	        date = self._parse_date(row.get('Date', ''))
   323	        matchday = row.get('Game Week')

   326	        except (ValueError, TypeError):

   328

   330	        venue = str(row.get('Match Venue', '')).strip()
   331	
   332	        # Look up existing match by teams + date, or by teams + matchday if no date
   333	        filters = {'home_team': home_team, 'away_team': away_team}
   334	        if date:
   335	            filters['date'] = date
   336	        else:
   337	            filters['matchday'] = matchday
   338	
   339	        filters['season'] = season
   340	        match = Match.objects.filter(**filters).first()
   341	
   342	        action = 'updated' if match else 'created'
   343

   347	            )
   348	            return {'action': action, 'goals': 0}
   349	
   350	        if not match:

   354	                away_team=away_team,
   355	                matchday=matchday,
   356	                date=date,
   357	                venue=venue,
   358	            )
   359	
   360	        match.season = season
   361	        match.status = Match.STATUS_UPCOMING
   362	        match.venue = venue or match.venue
   363	        match.matchday = matchday
   364	        match.date = date
   365	        match.kick_off = kick_off
   366	        match.home_score = None
   367	        match.away_score = None

    35	let refreshPromise: Promise<string> | null = null

    37	async function refreshAccessToken(): Promise<string> {
    38	  // Deduplicate — if multiple requests 401 at once, only one refresh fires
    39	  if (refreshPromise) return refreshPromise
    40	
    41	  refreshPromise = (async () => {
    42	    const refresh = tokens.getRefresh()
    43	    if (!refresh) throw new Error('No refresh token.')
    44	
    45	    const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/auth/refresh/`, {
    46	      method: 'POST',
    47	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    48	      body: JSON.stringify({ refresh }),
    49	    })
    50	
    51	    if (!res.ok) {
    52	      tokens.clear()
    53	      throw new Error('Session expired.')
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    const data = await res.json() as { access: string }
    57	    tokens.set(data.access, refresh)
    58	    return data.access
    59	  })()
    60	
    61	  try {
    62	    return await refreshPromise
    63	  } finally {
    64	    refreshPromise = null
    65	  }
    66	}

    68	// ── Core request helper ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    69	async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit, retry = true): Promise<T> {
    70	  const headers = new Headers(init?.headers)
    71	
    72	  if (!headers.has('Content-Type') && !(init?.body instanceof FormData)) {
    73	    headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json')
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  const access = tokens.getAccess()
    77	  if (access) headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${access}`)
    78	
    79	  const response = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, { ...init, headers })
    80	
    81	  // Silent refresh on 401 — retry once with new token
    82	  if (response.status === 401 && retry) {
    83	    try {
    84	      const newAccess = await refreshAccessToken()
    85	      const retryHeaders = new Headers(headers)
    86	      retryHeaders.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${newAccess}`)
    87	      return request<T>(path, { ...init, headers: retryHeaders }, false)
    88	    } catch {
    89	      // Refresh failed — clear tokens, let the auth guard redirect to login
    90	      tokens.clear()
    91	      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('auth:expired'))
    92	      throw new Error('Session expired. Please sign in again.')
    93	    }
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  if (response.status === 204) return undefined as T
    97	
    98	  if (!response.ok) {
    99	    const payload = await response
   100	      .json()
   101	      .catch(() => ({ detail: 'Request failed. Please try again.' }))
   102	    const err = new Error(payload.detail ?? payload.error ?? 'Request failed.') as Error & { status: number; data: Record<string, unknown> }
   103	    err.status = response.status
   104	    err.data = payload
   105	    throw err
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  return response.json() as Promise<T>
   109	}

   111	// DRF returns paginated { count, results: [] } — unwrap to plain array
   112	async function list<T>(path: string): Promise<T[]> {
   113	  const data = await request<{ results: T[] } | T[]>(path)
   114	  return Array.isArray(data) ? data : data.results

    10	function CountUp({ value }: { value: number }) {
    11	  const [display, setDisplay] = useState(0)

    13	  useEffect(() => {
    14	    const duration = 500
    15	    const start = performance.now()
    16	    const frame = (time: number) => {
    17	      const progress = Math.min((time - start) / duration, 1)
    18	      setDisplay(Math.round(value * progress))
    19	      if (progress < 1) requestAnimationFrame(frame)
    20	    }
    21	    requestAnimationFrame(frame)
    22	  }, [value])
    23	
    24	  return <span>{display}</span>
    25	}

    71	    logoUrl:   t.logo_url || null,
    72	    founded:   t.founded ?? null,
    73	    manager:   t.manager || null,
    74	    bio:       t.bio || '',
    75	    honours:   t.titles > 0 ? [`${t.titles}× NWFL Champions`] : [],
    76	    form:      standing?.form ?? [],
    77	    position:  standing ? null : null, // computed by caller after sort
    78	    points:    standing?.pts ?? 0,
    79	    w:         standing?.w  ?? 0,
    80	    d:         standing?.d  ?? 0,
    81	    l:         standing?.l  ?? 0,
    82	    gd:        standing?.gd ?? 0,
    83	    nextMatch: nextMatchMap[t.id] ?? null,
    84	  }
    85	}

    87	// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    88	
    89	export async function fetchStandings(season) {
    90	  const qs = season ? `?season=${encodeURIComponent(season)}` : ''
    91	  const rows = await get(`/api/standings/${qs}`)
    92	  const adapted = rows.map(adaptStanding)
    93	  const groupA = adapted.filter(s => {
    94	    const row = rows.find(r => r.team.id === s.id)
    95	    return row?.team.group === 'A'
    96	  })
    97	  const groupB = adapted.filter(s => {
    98	    const row = rows.find(r => r.team.id === s.id)
    99	    return row?.team.group === 'B'
   100	  })
   101	  // Sort by points desc, then gd desc
   102	  const sort = arr => [...arr].sort((a, b) => (b.pts - a.pts) || (b.gd - a.gd))
   103	  return { groupA: sort(groupA), groupB: sort(groupB) }
   104	}
   105	
   106	export async function fetchMatches(season) {
   107	  const qs = season ? `?season=${encodeURIComponent(season)}` : ''
   108	  const rows = await get(`/api/matches/${qs}`)
   109	  return rows.map(adaptMatch)
   110	}
   111	
   112	export async function fetchSeasons() {
   113	  return get('/api/matches/seasons/')
   114	}
   115	
   116	export async function fetchAnalyticsSeason() {
   117	  const rows = await get('/api/analytics/season/')
   118	  return rows
   119	}
   120	
   121	export async function fetchTeams(season) {
   122	  const qs = season ? `?season=${encodeURIComponent(season)}` : ''
   123	  const [teams, standings, matches] = await Promise.all([
   124	    get('/api/teams/'),
   125	    get(`/api/standings/${qs}`),
   126	    get(`/api/matches/${qs}`),
   127	  ])
   128	
   129	  // Build standings lookup by team id
   130	  const standingsMap = {}
   131	  for (const s of standings) {
   132	    standingsMap[s.team.id] = adaptStanding(s)
   133	  }
   134	
   135	  // Build "next upcoming match" label per team
   136	  const now = new Date()
   137	  const upcoming = matches
   138	    .filter(m => m.status === 'UPCOMING')
   139	    .sort((a, b) => {
   140	      if (!a.date && !b.date) return 0
   141	      if (!a.date) return 1
   142	      if (!b.date) return -1
   143	      return new Date(a.date) - new Date(b.date)
   144	    })
   145	
   146	  const nextMatchMap = {}
   147	  for (const m of upcoming) {
   148	    const homeId = m.home_team.id
   149	    const awayId = m.away_team.id
   150	    if (!nextMatchMap[homeId]) {
   151	      nextMatchMap[homeId] = `vs ${m.away_team.name} · MD ${m.matchday}`
   152	    }
   153	    if (!nextMatchMap[awayId]) {
   154	      nextMatchMap[awayId] = `vs ${m.home_team.name} · MD ${m.matchday}`
   155	    }
   156	  }
   157	
   158	  const adapted = teams.map(t => adaptTeam(t, standingsMap, nextMatchMap))
   159	
   160	  // Assign position within each group by points
   161	  const assignPositions = arr => {
   162	    const sorted = [...arr].sort((a, b) => (b.points - a.points) || (b.gd - a.gd))
   163	    return sorted.map((t, i) => ({ ...t, position: i + 1 }))
   164	  }
   165	
   166	  const groupA = assignPositions(adapted.filter(t => t.group === 'A'))
   167	  const groupB = assignPositions(adapted.filter(t => t.group === 'B'))
   168	
   169	  return [...groupA, ...groupB]
   170	}


</details>

{
  "path": "c:/work/the-nwfl/src/hooks/useSeason.js",
  "content": "import { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { fetchSeasons } from \"../lib/api\";\n\nexport function useSeason() {\n  const [season, setSeason] = useState(\"\");\n  const [seasons, setSeasons] = useState([]);\n  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fetchSeasons()\n      .then((list) => {\n        setSeasons(list);\n        if (list.length && !season) {\n          setSeason(list[0]);\n        }\n        setReady(true);\n      })\n      .catch(() => setReady(true));\n  }, []);\n\n  return { season, setSeason, seasons, ready };\n}\n"
}
```

(\n        <SkeletonTable />\n      ) : activeGroup === 'A' ? (\n        <Table data={groupA} relegateCount={RELEGATE_A} />\n      ) : (\n        <Table data={groupB} relegateCount={RELEGATE_B} />\n      )}\n\n      <div className=\"standings-table__legend\">\n        <span className=\"standings-table__legend-item standings-table__legend-item--qualify\">\n          Qualifies for Playoffs\n        </span>\n        <span className=\"standings-table__legend-item standings-table__legend-item--relegate\">\n          Relegated\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"

'upcoming' : 'final'}`}>\n          {isUpcoming ? 'Upcoming' : 'Full Time'}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Matchup row */}\n      <div className=\"fixtures-panel__matchup\">\n        {/* Home */}\n        <div className=\"fixtures-panel__team fixtures-panel__team--home\">\n          <TeamLogo logoUrl={match.homeLogoUrl} name={match.homeTeam} />\n          <span className=\"fixtures-panel__team-name\">{match.homeShortName}</span>\n          <span className=\"fixtures-panel__team-role\">Home</span>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Center */}\n        <div className=\"fixtures-panel__center\">\n          {isUpcoming || (match.homeScore == null && match.awayScore == null) ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"fixtures-panel__vs\">VS</span>\n              {match.time && <span className=\"fixtures-panel__time\">{match.time}</span>}\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"fixtures-panel__scoreline\">\n              <span className=\"fixtures-panel__score-num\">{match.homeScore ?? 0}</span>\n              <span className=\"fixtures-panel__score-sep\">:</span>\n              <span className=\"fixtures-panel__score-num\">{match.awayScore ?? 0}</span>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Away */}\n        <div className=\"fixtures-panel__team fixtures-panel__team--away\">\n          <TeamLogo logoUrl={match.awayLogoUrl} name={match.awayTeam} />\n          <span className=\"fixtures-panel__team-name\">{match.awayShortName}</span>\n          <span className=\"fixtures-panel__team-role\">Away</span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Footer: venue */}\n      {match.venue && (\n        <div className=\"fixtures-panel__footer\">\n          <svg width=\"12\" height=\"12\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth=\"2\" strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\">\n            <path d=\"M20 10c0 6-8 12-8 12s-8-6-8-12a8 8 0 0 1 16 0Z\" />\n            <circle cx=\"12\" cy=\"10\" r=\"3\" />\n          </svg>\n          <span className=\"fixtures-panel__venue\">{match.venue}</span>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function FixturesPanel({ matches = [], loading = false }) {\n  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('fixtures');\n\n  const fixtures = matches.filter(m => m.status === 'UPCOMING');\n  const results = matches.filter(m => m.status === 'FT');\n\n  const displayMatches = activeTab === 'fixtures' ? fixtures : results;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"fixtures-panel\">\n      <div className=\"fixtures-panel__header\">\n        <h2 className=\"fixtures-panel__title\">Fixtures &amp; Results</h2>\n        <div className=\"fixtures-panel__tabs\">\n          <button\n            className={`fixtures-panel__tab${activeTab === 'fixtures' ? ' active' : ''}`}\n            onClick={() => setActiveTab('fixtures')}\n          >\n            Fixtures ({fixtures.length})\n          </button>\n          <button\n            className={`fixtures-panel__tab${activeTab === 'results' ? ' active' : ''}`}\n            onClick={() => setActiveTab('results')}\n          >\n            Results ({results.length})\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"fixtures-panel__list\">\n        {loading ? (\n          <>\n            <SkeletonCard />\n            <SkeletonCard />\n            <SkeletonCard />\n            <SkeletonCard />\n            <SkeletonCard />\n          </>\n        ) : displayMatches.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"fixtures-panel__empty\">No {activeTab} available for this season.</p>\n        ) : (\n          displayMatches.map(match => (\n            <FixtureCard key={match.id} match={match} />\n          ))\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      <Link to=\"/stats\" className=\"fixtures-panel__cta\">\n        View All Fixtures\n      </Link>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"

     9	const GROUP_COLORS = { A: '#8A3DFF', B: '#6A2BD9' }
    10	
    11	// ── Custom tooltip shared across charts ──────────────────────────────────────
    12	function ChartTooltip({ active, payload, label, unit = '' }) {
    13	  if (!active || !payload?.length) return null
    14	  return (
    15	    <div className="analytics__tooltip">
    16	      <p className="analytics__tooltip-label">{label}</p>
    17	      {payload.map((p, i) => (
    18	        <p key={i} style={{ color: p.color ?? p.fill }}>
    19	          {p.name}: <strong>{p.value}{unit}</strong>
    20	        </p>
    21	      ))}
    22	    </div>
    23	  )
    24	}
    25	
    26	// ── Custom bar shape — draws a dash for zero, bar otherwise ──────────────────
    27	function GDBar(props) {
    28	  const { x, y, width, height, value, fill } = props
    29	  if (value === 0) {
    30	    const dashW = 16
    31	    const dashH = 3
    32	    return (
    33	      <rect
    34	        x={x}
    35	        y={y + height / 2 - dashH / 2}
    36	        width={dashW}
    37	        height={dashH}
    38	        fill="rgba(167,143,191,0.4)"
    39	        rx={2}
    40	      />
    41	    )
    42	  }
    43	  return <rect x={x} y={y} width={width} height={height} fill={fill} rx={4} />
    44	}
    45	
    46	// ── Goal Difference chart (from standings) ───────────────────────────────────
    47	function GoalDifferenceChart({ data }) {
    48	  const sorted = [...data].sort((a, b) => b.gd - a.gd)
    49	  return (
    50	    <div className="analytics__chart-wrap">
    51	      <h3 className="analytics__chart-title">Goal Difference</h3>
    52	      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={sorted.length * 36 + 60}>
    53	        <BarChart
    54	          data={sorted}
    55	          layout="vertical"
    56	          margin={{ top: 8, right: 40, left: 140, bottom: 8 }}
    57	        >
    58	          <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(138,61,255,0.12)" horizontal={false} />
    59	          <XAxis
    60	            type="number"
    61	            tick={{ fill: '#a78fbf', fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Poppins' }}
    62	            axisLine={{ stroke: 'rgba(138,61,255,0.2)' }}
    63	            tickLine={false}
    64	          />
    65	          <YAxis
    66	            type="category"
    67	            dataKey="name"
    68	            width={135}
    69	            tick={{ fill: '#f0e6ff', fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Poppins' }}
    70	            axisLine={false}
    71	            tickLine={false}
    72	          />
    73	          <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip unit="" />} cursor={{ fill: 'rgba(138,61,255,0.08)' }} />
    74	          <Bar
    75	            dataKey="gd"
    76	            name="Goal Diff"
    77	            shape={(props) => (
    78	              <GDBar {...props} fill={props.value >= 0 ? '#8A3DFF' : '#ef4444'} />
    79	            )}
    80	          />
    81	        </BarChart>
    82	      </ResponsiveContainer>
    83	    </div>
    84	  )
    85	}
    86	
    87	// ── Clean Sheets chart (from season stats) ───────────────────────────────────
    88	function CleanSheetsChart({ data }) {

    90	  return (

    94	        <BarChart
    95	          data={sorted}
    96	          layout="vertical"
    97	          margin={{ top: 8, right: 40, left: 140, bottom: 8 }}
    98	        >
    99	          <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(138,61,255,0.12)" horizontal={false} />
   100	          <XAxis
   101	            type="number"
   102	            allowDecimals={false}
   103	            tick={{ fill: '#a78fbf', fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Poppins' }}
   104	            axisLine={{ stroke: 'rgba(138,61,255,0.2)' }}

   116	          <Bar dataKey="clean_sheets" name="Clean Sheets" radius={[0, 4, 4, 0]}>
   117	            {sorted.map((entry, i) => (

   128	function GoalAveragesChart({ data }) {

   178	// ── Page ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   179	export default function Analytics() {

   204	        return match?.group === group

   206	
   207	  const hasSeasonData = seasonStats.length > 0
   208	
   209	  return (

   223	            >
   224	              {g === 'ALL' ? 'All Teams' : `Group ${g}`}
   225	            </button>
   226	          ))}

   247	          </div>

The user hasn't said anything new. Let me summarize all the changes I've made across the three active tasks:

1. **Standing model schema bug** — Changed `OneToOneField` to `ForeignKey` with `unique_together=['team','season']`. Migrations applied successfully.

2. **Standings auto-calc** — Updated `recalculate_standings` command to be season-aware (accepts `--season` arg, processes each season separately). Added `post_save` signal on `Match` to auto-recalculate standings when a result is saved.

3. **HTHG/HTAG** — Added `home_ht_score` and `away_ht_score` to `Match` model, serializers, sync script, and admin MatchForm.

4. **Fixtures/schedule separation** — Updated public frontend `FixturesPanel` to show Fixtures and Results in separate tabs. Updated `fetchMatches` to accept season filter and created `useSeason` hook.

All five active issues are now resolved. Here's what was changed across the backend, admin, and public frontend:

---

### 1. Standing Model Schema Bug
**File:** `standings/models.py` + migration `0002`
- Changed `team = OneToOneField(Team)` → `ForeignKey(Team)` 
- Added `unique_together = ['team', 'season']`
- Updated `Meta.ordering` to `['-points', '-gf', 'ga']`
- Migration applied cleanly

### 2. Standings Auto-Calculation (Season-Aware)
**Files:** `standings/management/commands/recalculate_standings.py`, `matches/signals.py`, `matches/apps.py`
- Recalculate command now iterates per distinct season and writes `(team, season)` rows
- Added `--season` arg to target a single season
- Created `post_save` signal on `Match` that auto-recalculates standings for that match's season whenever a `FT` result is saved
- Registered signal in `MatchesConfig.ready()`

### 3. HTHG / HTAG (Half-Time Scores)
**Files:** `matches/models.py`, `matches/serializers.py`, `matches/management/commands/sync_from_sheet.py`, admin `MatchForm.tsx`, `types/index.ts`, `lib/api.ts`
- Added `home_ht_score` and `away_ht_score` fields to `Match` model
- Added to both `MatchSerializer` and `MatchListSerializer`
- Sync script reads `HTHG`/`HTAG` columns from sheet and passes `season` to `recalculate_standings`
- Admin MatchForm updated with HT score inputs

### 4. Fixtures vs Results Separation
**Files:** `src/components/FixturesPanel/FixturesPanel.jsx|.scss`, `src/components/StandingsTable/StandingsTable.jsx`, `src/components/LeagueSection/LeagueSection.jsx`, `src/hooks/useSeason.js`, `src/pages/Home/Home.jsx`, `src/pages/Teams/Teams.jsx`
- Created `useSeason` hook that fetches available seasons and defaults to latest
- Home & Teams pages now pass `season` to all API calls
- `FixturesPanel` shows **Fixtures** and **Results** as toggle tabs instead of one mixed list
- `StandingsTable` accepts standings as props rather than fetching internally

### 5. Dashboard Match Count Fix
**File:** `nwfl-admin/src/pages/Dashboard/index.tsx`
- Dashboard now loads seasons first, then fetches `api.getMatches({ season })` instead of all 557 matches
- Added season selector dropdown above stat cards

---

**Backend migrations applied:** `matches.0008` + `standings.0002`

## Analysis: Why Matches Stay as UPCOMING

### 1. `_parse_remarks()` is too strict — the primary bottleneck

**`matches/management/commands/sync_from_sheet.py:168-175`**
```python
def _parse_remarks(self, remarks):
    r = str(remarks).strip().lower()
    if r in ('completed', 'walkover', 'wo'):
        return Match.STATUS_FT
    if r in ('postponed', 'pending', 'abandoned', 'annulled', 'no contest', 'nc'):
        return Match.STATUS_PENDING
    return Match.STATUS_UPCOMING
```

This is the **single gatekeeper** for match status. It only recognizes three exact strings as completed.  
**Anything else — empty cell, `"FT"`, `"Full Time"`, `"Done"`, a typo — becomes `UPCOMING`.**

### 2. `_process_row()` blindly overwrites status on every sync

**`sync_from_sheet.py:273-278`**
```python
match.home_score = home_score
match.away_score = away_score
match.home_ht_score = home_ht_score
match.away_ht_score = away_ht_score
match.status = status        # <-- overwrites existing FT status every time
```

Even if a match was previously marked `FT` (e.g. by `update_scores.py` or a prior import), a subsequent sync with an empty or changed **Remarks** cell will **downgrade it back to `UPCOMING`**. There is no protection for already-completed matches.

### 3. `_process_row()` does **not** infer status from scores

If the sheet has valid `FTHG` and `FTAG` values but the **Remarks** cell is empty, the match stays `UPCOMING` with scores in the database. The code never checks *“do scores exist → this must be FT”*.

### 4. `_process_fixture_row()` destroys completed data

**`sync_from_sheet.py:372-379`**
```python
match.status = Match.STATUS_UPCOMING
match.home_score = None
match.away_score = None
match.save()
```

If anyone runs `sync_from_sheet --fixtures` against the same worksheet (or a sheet with overlapping matches), it finds existing matches by `home_team + away_team + date/matchday` and **wipes their scores and forces `UPCOMING`**.

### 5. `import_from_sheet.py` skips anything not explicitly “completed”

**`matches/management/commands/import_from_sheet.py:198-201`**
```python
if remarks not in ('completed', 'walkover'):
    skipped_incomplete += 1
    continue
```

This command (the one documented in `AGENTS.md`) **only imports rows whose Remarks cell is exactly `"completed"` or `"walkover"`**. If the sheet has scores but empty/non-standard remarks, those rows are skipped entirely and never become `FT`.

---

## Recommended Fix

1. **Infer FT from scores when remarks is ambiguous**  
   In `_process_row()`, if `FTHG` and `FTAG` are both valid integers, default the status to `FT` regardless of what Remarks says.

2. **Widen `_parse_remarks()`**  
   Add `"ft"`, `"full time"`, `"finished"`, `"done"` to the FT mapping. Also treat an empty remarks cell as `FT` **when scores are present**.

3. **Protect existing completed matches**  
   In `_process_row()`, if the existing match already has `status == FT` and valid scores, don’t overwrite it to `UPCOMING` unless an explicit `--force` flag is passed.

4. **Fix `_process_fixture_row()`**  
   If an existing match already has `status == FT` and non-null scores, skip it or at least preserve the scores/status instead of forcing `UPCOMING` / `None`.

5. **Apply the same logic to `import_from_sheet.py`**  
   Import rows that have valid `FTHG`/`FTAG` even if Remarks is empty, and set them to `FT`.

</details>

## Analysis: HTTP 413 on Batch Logo Uploads

### Root Cause

**Nginx is enforcing its default `client_max_body_size` of 1 MB**, and the repo's configuration that raises this to 20 MB is either not deployed or not active.

### Evidence

| Layer | Finding |
|-------|---------|
| **Django settings** (`core/settings.py`) | `DATA_UPLOAD_MAX_MEMORY_SIZE` and `FILE_UPLOAD_MAX_MEMORY_SIZE` are **not set**, so they fall back to Django defaults (2.5 MB each). Django returns **400** for these, **not 413**, so this is not the source of the error. |
| **Nginx repo config** (`nginx/nwfl-api.conf`) | `client_max_body_size 20M;` is present. |
| **Nginx deploy step** (`.github/workflows/deploy.yml`) | **Missing.** The workflow copies the systemd service file but **never copies or reloads the nginx config**. |
| **Gunicorn** (`nwfl-api.service`) | No body-size limits configured. Gunicorn does not enforce request body size by default. |
| **Media library views** (`media_library/views.py`) | Standard DRF `MultiPartParser` with chunked file handling. No size limits imposed here. |

### Why it fails on 17 logos

17 PNGs × ~100–500 KB ≈ **1.7–8.5 MB** per batch request.  
If Nginx is still running with its default `client_max_body_size 1M`, every batch request exceeding 1 MB gets an immediate **HTTP 413** before the request ever reaches Gunicorn or Django.

### Recommended Fixes

**1. Fix the active Nginx config on EC2 (immediate)**
```bash
# Check what limit is actually loaded
sudo nginx -T | grep client_max_body_size

# If it's missing or 1M, update the active site config
sudo cp ~/nwfl-backend/nginx/nwfl-api.conf /etc/nginx/sites-available/nwfl-api
sudo ln -sf /etc/nginx/sites-available/nwfl-api /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/
sudo nginx -t && sudo systemctl reload nginx
```

**2. Automate Nginx config deployment**  
Add to `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` (after the systemd service copy):
```yaml
echo "Updating Nginx config..."
sudo cp ~/nwfl-backend/nginx/nwfl-api.conf /etc/nginx/sites-available/nwfl-api
sudo ln -sf /etc/nginx/sites-available/nwfl-api /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/
sudo nginx -t && sudo systemctl reload nginx
```

**3. Explicit Django upload limits (belt-and-suspenders)**  
Add to `core/settings.py`:
```python
# Keep parsed POST data under 10 MB in memory; stream larger bodies to disk
DATA_UPLOAD_MAX_MEMORY_SIZE = 10 * 1024 * 1024   # 10 MB

# Stream uploaded files to disk once they exceed 5 MB
FILE_UPLOAD_MAX_MEMORY_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024    # 5 MB
```

### Verdict

The 413 is coming from **Nginx**, not Django or Gunicorn. The repo contains the correct `client_max_body_size 20M`, but the deploy pipeline does not apply it to the EC2 instance, leaving the 1 MB default in effect.

</details>

     1	"""
     2	Import all completed match results from the nwfl-regular HTML export.
     3	Reads FTHG/FTAG scores from the sheet, creates or updates match records,
     4	then wipes and recalculates standings and season stats.
     5	
     6	Usage:
     7	  uv run python manage.py import_from_sheet <path_to_html>
     8	  uv run python manage.py import_from_sheet <path_to_html> --dry-run
     9	  uv run python manage.py import_from_sheet <path_to_html> --season 2024/25
    10	"""
    11	
    12	from html.parser import HTMLParser
    13	from datetime import datetime
    14	from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError
    15	from django.db import transaction
    16	from matches.models import Match

    19	from analytics.models import TeamSeasonStat

    22	# ── HTML parser ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    23	
    24	class TableExtractor(HTMLParser):
    25	    def __init__(self):
    26	        super().__init__()
    27	        self.rows = []
    28	        self.current_row = []
    29	        self.current_cell = ''
    30	        self.in_td = False
    31	
    32	    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
    33	        if tag == 'tr':
    34	            self.current_row = []
    35	        elif tag in ('td', 'th'):
    36	            self.in_td = True
    37	            self.current_cell = ''
    38	
    39	    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
    40	        if tag in ('td', 'th'):
    41	            self.current_row.append(self.current_cell.strip())
    42	            self.in_td = False
    43	        elif tag == 'tr':
    44	            if self.current_row:
    45	                self.rows.append(self.current_row)
    46	
    47	    def handle_data(self, data):
    48	        if self.in_td:
    49	            self.current_cell += data
    50	
    51	
    52	# ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    53	
    54	def parse_date(raw):
    55	    raw = raw.strip()
    56	    for fmt in ('%d/%m/%Y', '%d/%m/%y', '%Y-%m-%d'):
    57	        try:
    58	            return datetime.strptime(raw, fmt).date()
    59	        except ValueError:
    60	            continue
    61	    return None
    62	
    63	
    64	def resolve_team(name, cache):
    65	    name = name.strip().lower()
    66	    # Direct hit
    67	    if name in cache:
    68	        return cache[name]
    69	    # Substring match
    70	    for key, team in cache.items():
    71	        if name in key or key in name:
    72	            return team
    73	    return None
    74	
    75	
    76	def recalculate_standings(completed_matches, team_cache, stdout, dry_run):
    77	    stats = {t.id: {'team': t, 'played': 0, 'won': 0, 'drawn': 0, 'lost': 0, 'gf': 0, 'ga': 0}
    78	             for t in team_cache.values()}
    79	
    80	    for m in completed_matches:
    81	        hs, ag = m.home_score or 0, m.away_score or 0
    82	        hid, aid = m.home_team_id, m.away_team_id
    83	        if hid not in stats or aid not in stats:
    84	            continue
    85	        stats[hid]['played'] += 1; stats[hid]['gf'] += hs; stats[hid]['ga'] += ag
    86	        stats[aid]['played'] += 1; stats[aid]['gf'] += ag; stats[aid]['ga'] += hs
    87	        if hs > ag:
    88	            stats[hid]['won'] += 1; stats[aid]['lost'] += 1
    89	        elif hs < ag:
    90	            stats[aid]['won'] += 1; stats[hid]['lost'] += 1
    91	        else:
    92	            stats[hid]['drawn'] += 1; stats[aid]['drawn'] += 1
    93	
    94	    if not dry_run:
    95	        for s in stats.values():
    96	            if s['played'] == 0:
    97	                continue
    98	            pts = s['won'] * 3 + s['drawn']
    99	            standing, _ = Standing.objects.get_or_create(team=s['team'])
   100	            standing.played = s['played']
   101	            standing.won = s['won']
   102	            standing.drawn = s['drawn']
   103	            standing.lost = s['lost']
   104	            standing.gf = s['gf']
   105	            standing.ga = s['ga']
   106	            standing.points = pts
   107	            standing.save()
   108	    stdout.write(f'  Standings recalculated for {sum(1 for s in stats.values() if s["played"] > 0)} teams')
   109	
   110	
   111	def recalculate_season_stats(completed_matches, team_cache, season, stdout, dry_run):
   112	    stats = {t.id: {'team': t, 'played': 0, 'gf': 0, 'ga': 0, 'cs': 0}
   113	             for t in team_cache.values()}
   114	
   115	    for m in completed_matches:
   116	        hs, ag = m.home_score or 0, m.away_score or 0
   117	        hid, aid = m.home_team_id, m.away_team_id
   118	        if hid not in stats or aid not in stats:
   119	            continue
   120	        stats[hid]['played'] += 1; stats[hid]['gf'] += hs; stats[hid]['ga'] += ag
   121	        stats[aid]['played'] += 1; stats[aid]['gf'] += ag; stats[aid]['ga'] += hs
   122	        if ag == 0: stats[hid]['cs'] += 1
   123	        if hs == 0: stats[aid]['cs'] += 1
   124	
   125	    if not dry_run:
   126	        for s in stats.values():
   127	            if s['played'] == 0:
   128	                continue
   129	            p = s['played']
   130	            obj, _ = TeamSeasonStat.objects.get_or_create(team=s['team'])
   131	            obj.season = season
   132	            obj.avg_goals_scored = round(s['gf'] / p, 2)
   133	            obj.avg_goals_conceded = round(s['ga'] / p, 2)
   134	            obj.clean_sheets = s['cs']
   135	            obj.save()
   136	    stdout.write(f'  Season stats computed for {sum(1 for s in stats.values() if s["played"] > 0)} teams')
   137

   139	# ── Command ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   140	
   141	class Command(BaseCommand):
   142	    help = 'Import completed match results from the nwfl-regular Google Sheets HTML export'
   143	
   144	    def add_arguments(self, parser):
   145	        parser.add_argument('html_file', type=str, help='Path to the nwfl-regular.html export')
   146	        parser.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true', help='Parse without writing to DB')
   147	        parser.add_argument('--season', type=str, default=None,
   148	                            help='Only import this season e.g. 2024/25 (imports all if omitted)')
   149	        parser.add_argument('--wipe-existing', action='store_true',
   150	                            help='Delete all existing match records before importing (fresh start)')
   151	
   152	    def handle(self, *args, **options):
   153	        html_path = options['html_file']
   154	        dry_run = options['dry_run']
   155	        season_filter = options['season']
   156	        wipe = options['wipe_existing']
   157	
   158	        # ── Parse HTML ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   159	        try:
   160	            with open(html_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
   161	                content = f.read()
   162	        except FileNotFoundError:
   163	            raise CommandError(f'File not found: {html_path}')
   164	
   165	        parser = TableExtractor()

   167	
   168	        # Row 1 = column letters, Row 2 = headers, Row 3 = empty, data starts row 4
   169	        data_rows = [r for r in parser.rows[3:] if len(r) > 13 and r[8] and r[9]]
   170	        self.stdout.write(f'Parsed {len(data_rows)} data rows from HTML')
   171	
   172	        # ── Build team cache ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   173	        team_cache = {}
   174	        for team in Team.objects.all():
   175	            team_cache[team.name.lower()] = team
   176	            team_cache[team.short_name.lower()] = team
   177	
   178	        # ── Filter and validate rows ──────────────────────────────────────────
   179	        valid = []
   180	        skipped_season = 0
   181	        skipped_incomplete = 0
   182	
   183	        for i, row in enumerate(data_rows):

   186	            gw_raw    = row[4].strip()

   190	            fthg_raw  = row[12].strip()
   191	            ftag_raw  = row[13].strip()
   192	            remarks   = row[15].strip().lower() if len(row) > 15 else ''
   193	
   194	            if season_filter and season != season_filter:
   195	                skipped_season += 1
   196	                continue
   197	
   198	            # Only completed or walkover
   199	            if remarks not in ('completed', 'walkover'):
   200	                skipped_incomplete += 1
   201	                continue
   202	
   203	            # Must have scores
   204	            try:

   206	                ftag = int(ftag_raw)
   207	            except (ValueError, TypeError):

   209	                continue
   210	
   211	            try:

   215	                continue
   216

   220	            if not home or not away:

   243

   246	            return

   249	        with transaction.atomic():

   252	                self.stdout.write(f'  Wiped {deleted} existing match records')
   253	
   254	            created = updated = already_correct = 0
   255

All backend code fixes are done and compile cleanly. Here's the honest picture:

---

## What I Fixed

### 1. Match Status Sync (`sync_from_sheet.py`)
**Root causes:**
- `_parse_remarks()` only recognized `"completed"` / `"walkover"` — empty remarks cells downgraded matches to `UPCOMING`
- Every sync run **blindly overwrote** existing `FT` status back to `UPCOMING`
- Fixtures sync wiped scores/status of completed matches

**Fixes applied:**
- `_parse_remarks()` now recognizes `"ft"`, `"full time"`, `"finished"`, `"done"`
- `_process_row()` now **infers `FT` from scores** when remarks are ambiguous
- Existing `FT` matches with scores are **protected** from being downgraded
- Fixtures sync now **skips** matches that are already completed

### 2. HTML Import (`import_from_sheet.py`)
- Same strict remark check fixed — now accepts rows with valid scores even if remarks is empty
- Fixed season-aware standing writes (`get_or_create(team, season)` instead of just `team`)
- Fixed `TeamSeasonStat` model's `OneToOneField` → `ForeignKey` + `unique_together` (same bug as Standing)

### 3. HTTP 413 Upload Error
**Root cause:** The deploy workflow never copies the nginx config to EC2. Nginx is running with its **default 1 MB limit**.

**Fixes applied:**
- Added nginx config copy/reload to `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`
- Added Django upload limits (`15 MB` / `10 MB`) to `core/settings.py`

---

## The Data Problem

I checked the local DB — there are **112 UPCOMING matches in 2024/25 with no scores at all**. The sync script can't invent scores. This means the **Google Sheet `nwfl-regular` tab either:**

1. Doesn't have `FTHG` / `FTAG` values filled in for most rows, or
2. The data was imported from the fixtures tab (which has no scores) and never overwritten by the regular tab

**The code fixes prevent future corruption, but the missing scores must come from the sheet.**

---

## What You Need To Do

**For the 413 error (immediate):**
Push to `main`. The deploy workflow will now copy `client_max_body_size 20M` to the EC2 nginx config and reload it.

**For the match data:**
1. Check the Google Sheet `nwfl-regular` tab — make sure `FTHG` and `FTAG` columns have values for completed matches
2. After deploying, SSH into EC2 and run:
   ```bash
   cd ~/nwfl-backend
   source venv/bin/activate
   python manage.py sync_from_sheet --season 2024/25
   ```
   The new logic will infer `FT` status from any rows that have scores.

If the sheet itself is missing scores, you'll need to enter them in the sheet first (or manually in the admin dashboard), then re-sync.

## NWFL Backend — AI Team Parsing Analysis

### 1. Where `/api/internal/parse-team/` is defined

**URL routing:** `c:/work/nwfl-backend/core/urls.py` (line 101)
```python
path('api/internal/parse-team/', parse_team_ai, name='parse_team_ai')
```

**View function:** `c:/work/nwfl-backend/teams/views.py` (lines 149-182)
```python
def parse_team_ai(request):
    text = request.data.get('text', '').strip() or None
    image = request.FILES.get('image')
    ...
    result = parse_team(text=text, image_path=image_path)
    ...
```

---

### 2. What AI service/model is used

**Service:** **OpenRouter** (`https://openrouter.ai/api`)  
**SDK:** `anthropic` Python client (configured with `base_url="https://openrouter.ai/api"`)

**File:** `c:/work/nwfl-backend/etl/services/ai_parser.py`

- **Text model** (line 26): `os.getenv("AI_MODEL", "openrouter/free")`
- **Vision models** (lines 30-45): Fallback list of free models:
  - `google/gemma-4-31b-it:free`
  - `google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free`
  - `nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning:free`
  - `google/gemma-3-27b-it:free`

The client is initialized lazily in `_get_client()` (lines 11-23) with **no explicit HTTP timeout** (`timeout=anthropic.NOT_GIVEN`).

---

### 3. What prompt/template is used

**Prompt file:** `c:/work/nwfl-backend/etl/souls/team_parser.md` ("Soul: Team Data Parser")

It's a system prompt that tells the AI:
- It is a data entry assistant for the NWFL admin portal.
- It receives raw text, screenshots, or webpage extracts.
- It must **only extract** structured data from the input and return JSON.

**Key output schema (line 59-71):**
```json
{
  "name": "Full official team name",
  "short_name": "Short display name",
  "slug": "url-slug",
  "city": "City name or null",
  "state": "State name or null",
  "group": "A" or "B" or null,
  "founded": integer or null,
  "manager": "Manager/coach full name or null",
  "titles": integer,
  "bio": "1-3 sentence summary ... otherwise empty string",
  "confidence": "HIGH" | "MEDIUM" | "LOW"
}
```

**Crucially**, the prompt explicitly instructs the AI to return **`null`** or **`""`** for missing fields. It does **not** tell the AI to research or look up missing data. It only extracts what is present in the provided input.

---

### 4. How missing fields are handled

**Current behavior:** The AI returns `null` / empty string when data isn't in the input. There is **no automatic web research** inside `parse_team_ai`.

There is a **separate** research pipeline that already exists but is **not connected** to `parse-team/`:

- **`research_teams` API endpoint:** `c:/work/nwfl-backend/teams/views.py` (lines 191-217)
- **`research_team` function:** `c:/work/nwfl-backend/etl/services/team_scraper.py` (lines 240-298)

`research_team` does the following:
1. Generates Wikipedia URL candidates from the team name.
2. Searches DuckDuckGo for `"{team_name} Nigeria women football NWFL"`.
3. Scrapes up to 6 candidate URLs with Playwright (20s page timeout) or `requests` (12s timeout).
4. Extracts text from the page.
5. Feeds the text into the **same** `parse_team()` AI function.
6. Returns the first result with `confidence != LOW` and a valid name.

---

### Root Causes of the User's Issues

| Issue | Cause |
|-------|-------|
| **500 error / 30s timeout** | The `anthropic` client has **no timeout set** (`timeout=NOT_GIVEN`). Free OpenRouter models can be extremely slow or hang. The view catches `VisionUnavailableError` (returns 503) but any other slow-response/timeout from the SDK raises a generic exception → 500. |
| **Empty fields (City, State, Manager, Founded, Bio)** | The prompt explicitly tells the AI to return `null`/`""` for missing fields. The AI is **not asked to research** — it only extracts from the raw text/image the user uploads. If the user input is sparse, the result is sparse. |

---

### Changes Needed to Make the AI Do Web Research for Missing Fields

The cleanest fix is to **chain the existing `research_team` scraper into `parse_team_ai`** as a fallback enrichment step.

#### Option A: Automatic fallback research inside `parse_team_ai` (Recommended)

**In `c:/work/nwfl-backend/teams/views.py`**, modify `parse_team_ai` (around line 168) to:

1. Get the initial AI parse result.
2. If `confidence` is `LOW` **or** critical fields (`city`, `state`, `manager`, `founded`, `bio`) are missing/empty, automatically call `research_team(result["name"])`.
3. Merge the research result into the original, preferring non-empty values.
4. Return the enriched result.

Example merge logic:
```python
from etl.services.team_scraper import research_team

# After initial parse
if isinstance(result, dict):
    result = [result]

for r in result:
    if r.get("name") and (r.get("confidence") == "LOW" or not all([
        r.get("city"), r.get("state"), r.get("manager"), r.get("founded"), r.get("bio")
    ])):
        try:
            enriched = research_team(r["name"])
            # Merge: keep original non-null values, fill gaps from research
            for key in ["city", "state", "manager", "founded", "bio", "titles"]:
                if not r.get(key) and enriched.get(key):
                    r[key] = enriched[key]
            r["confidence"] = "HIGH"  # or enriched["confidence"]
        except Exception:
            pass  # Keep original parse result even if research fails
```

#### Option B: Add explicit AI HTTP timeout

**In `c:/work/nwfl-backend/etl/services/ai_parser.py`**, add a timeout to the client (line 15):
```python
_client = anthropic.Anthropic(
    api_key=os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY"),
    base_url="https://openrouter.ai/api",
    timeout=20.0,  # Hard cap at 20 seconds
    ...
)
```

#### Option C: Update the prompt to flag missing fields

**In `c:/work/nwfl-backend/etl/souls/team_parser.md`**, change the instructions for missing fields from:
```
"city": "City name or null",
```
to something like:
```
"city": "City name, or the string '__MISSING__' if not found in the input",
```
Then the view can detect `__MISSING__` and trigger research. (Less robust than Option A.)

#### Option D: Add a `research=true` query parameter

Let the frontend opt-in to the slower research path by passing `?research=1`, so simple parses stay fast and only deliberate requests trigger the full web-scraping pipeline.

---

### Summary of Key Files

| File | Role |
|------|------|
| `c:/work/nwfl-backend/core/urls.py` | URL routing for `/api/internal/parse-team/` |
| `c:/work/nwfl-backend/teams/views.py` | `parse_team_ai` view (entry point) |
| `c:/work/nwfl-backend/etl/services/ai_parser.py` | `_call_ai()`, `parse_team()`, OpenRouter client config |
| `c:/work/nwfl-backend/etl/souls/team_parser.md` | The system prompt / "soul" for team parsing |
| `c:/work/nwfl-backend/etl/services/team_scraper.py` | `research_team()` — web scraping + AI re-parse (currently unused by `parse-team/`) |
| `c:/work/nwfl-backend/teams/models.py` | `Team` model schema |

**Recommended minimal change:** Add `timeout=20.0` to the Anthropic client in `ai_parser.py`, and wire `research_team()` as a fallback inside `parse_team_ai` in `teams/views.py` when critical fields are empty.

    32	@api_view(['POST'])

    34	def check_teams_exist(request):
    35	    """
    36	    Given a list of team names from AI output, check which already exist in the DB.
    37	    Uses fuzzy matching: checks name, short_name, and slug against each candidate.
    38	
    39	    Body: { "teams": ["Rivers Angels FC", "Edo Queens FC", ...] }

    45	
    46	    result = {}

    48	
    49	    for name in names:
    50	        name_clean = name.strip().lower()
    51	        # Strip common suffixes for comparison
    52	        for suffix in [' fc', ' football club', ' ladies fc', ' ladies']:
    53	            name_clean = name_clean.removesuffix(suffix)
    54	
    55	        match = None
    56	        for team in all_teams:
    57	            candidates = [

    61	            ]

    89	@api_view(['GET'])
    90	@permission_classes([IsAdminUser])
    91	def teams_missing_logos(request):
    92	    """
    93	    Find teams without logos and suggest images from the media library.
    94	    Suggestions are based on tag matching (team name, slug, short_name).
    95	    """
    96	    missing = Team.objects.filter(Q(logo='') | Q(logo__isnull=True))
    97	    all_images = MediaImage.objects.all()
    98	
    99	    result = []
   100	    for team in missing:
   101	        # Build search terms for this team
   102	        terms = [
   103	            team.name.lower(),
   104	            team.short_name.lower(),
   105	            team.slug.lower().replace('-', ' '),
   106	            team.slug.lower(),
   107	        ]
   108	
   109	        suggested = []
   110	        for img in all_images:
   111	            img_tags = [t.lower() for t in img.tags]
   112	            score = 0
   113	            for term in terms:
   114	                if term in img_tags:
   115	                    score += 3
   116	                if term in img.filename.lower():
   117	                    score += 2
   118	                if any(term in tag for tag in img_tags):
   119	                    score += 1
   120	            if score > 0:
   121	                suggested.append({
   122	                    'image': MediaImageSerializer(img, context={'request': request}).data,
   123	                    'score': score,
   124	                })
   125	
   126	        # Sort by relevance score, highest first
   127	        suggested.sort(key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)
   128	
   129	        result.append({
   130	            'team': TeamSerializer(team, context={'request': request}).data,
   131	            'suggestions': [s['image'] for s in suggested[:5]],
   132	        })
   133	
   134	    return Response({
   135	        'missing_count': missing.count(),
   136	        'teams': result,
   137	    })

   149	def parse_team_ai(request):

   151	    image = request.FILES.get('image')
   152	
   153	    if not text and not image:
   154	        return Response({'error': 'Provide text or image.'}, status=400)
   155

   162	            tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False, suffix=suffix)
   163	            for chunk in image.chunks():

   165	            tmp.close()
   166	            image_path = tmp.name
   167

   171	        return Response(result)

   174	    except Exception as e:
   175	        return Response({'error': str(e)}, status=500)
   176	    finally:

     1	"""
     2	Playwright-based team researcher.
     3	
     4	Strategy (in order):
     5	  1. Try Wikipedia directly via predictable URL slug
     6	  2. Search DDG with broad query, prefer Wikipedia/football-db domains
     7	  3. For each candidate URL, scrape with Playwright + extract text
     8	  4. Pass text (+screenshot if available) to team_parser AI soul
     9	  5. Accept the result only if confidence is MEDIUM or HIGH and name is non-null
    10	  6. Raise ValueError if nothing usable is found
    11	"""
    12	
    13	import re
    14	import tempfile
    15	import urllib.parse
    16	from pathlib import Path
    17	
    18	import requests
    19	from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
    20	from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright, TimeoutError as PWTimeout
    21	
    22	from .ai_parser import parse_team
    23	
    24	
    25	_HEADERS = {
    26	    "User-Agent": (
    27	        "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) "
    28	        "AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) "
    29	        "Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36"
    30	    )
    31	}
    32	
    33	_PREFER_DOMAINS = [
    34	    "en.wikipedia.org",
    35	    "en.m.wikipedia.org",
    36	    "soccerdonna.de",
    37	    "transfermarkt.com",
    38	    "soccerway.com",
    39	    "nff.com.ng",
    40	    "nwfl.com.ng",
    41	    "nigeriafootball.ng",
    42	    "goal.com",
    43	]
    44	
    45	_SKIP_DOMAINS = [
    46	    "youtube.com", "twitter.com", "x.com", "instagram.com",
    47	    "facebook.com", "tiktok.com", "reddit.com",
    48	]
    49	
    50	
    51	# ── Wikipedia direct-probe ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    52	
    53	def _wikipedia_slug(team_name: str) -> list[str]:
    54	    """
    55	    Generate likely Wikipedia article slugs for a team name.
    56	    e.g. "Naija Ratels FC" → ["Naija_Ratels_F.C.", "Naija_Ratels_FC", "Naija_Ratels"]
    57	    """
    58	    base = team_name.strip()
    59	    variants = []
    60	    # "FC" → "F.C."
    61	    with_dots = re.sub(r'\bFC\b', 'F.C.', base).replace(' ', '_')
    62	    variants.append(with_dots)
    63	    # plain replace
    64	    plain = base.replace(' ', '_')
    65	    if plain != with_dots:
    66	        variants.append(plain)
    67	    # strip FC / Ladies FC suffix
    68	    stripped = re.sub(r'\s+(FC|F\.C\.|Ladies FC|Ladies)$', '', base).replace(' ', '_')
    69	    if stripped not in variants:
    70	        variants.append(stripped)
    71	    return variants
    72	
    73	
    74	def _try_wikipedia_direct(team_name: str) -> list[str]:
    75	    """Return Wikipedia URLs to try, most-likely first."""
    76	    urls = []
    77	    for slug in _wikipedia_slug(team_name):
    78	        urls.append(f"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/{slug}")
    79	    return urls
    80	
    81	
    82	# ── DDG search ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    83	
    84	def _ddg_search(query: str, max_results: int = 10) -> list[str]:
    85	    """DuckDuckGo HTML search — no API key needed."""
    86	    try:
    87	        resp = requests.post(
    88	            "https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/",
    89	            data={"q": query, "b": "", "kl": "wt-wt"},
    90	            headers=_HEADERS,
    91	            timeout=12,
    92	        )
    93	        resp.raise_for_status()
    94	    except Exception:
    95	        return []
    96	
    97	    soup = BeautifulSoup(resp.text, "html.parser")
    98	    urls: list[str] = []
    99	    for a in soup.select("a.result__url, a.result__a"):
   100	        href = a.get("href", "")
   101	        if "uddg=" in href:
   102	            m = re.search(r"uddg=([^&]+)", href)
   103	            if m:
   104	                href = urllib.parse.unquote(m.group(1))
   105	        if href.startswith("http") and len(urls) < max_results:
   106	            urls.append(href)
   107	    return urls
   108	
   109	
   110	def _rank_urls(urls: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   111	    def score(u: str) -> int:
   112	        for skip in _SKIP_DOMAINS:
   113	            if skip in u:
   114	                return 999
   115	        for i, domain in enumerate(_PREFER_DOMAINS):
   116	            if domain in u:
   117	                return i
   118	        return len(_PREFER_DOMAINS)
   119	    return sorted(dict.fromkeys(urls), key=score)  # deduplicate + rank
   120	
   121	
   122	def _candidate_urls(team_name: str) -> list[str]:
   123	    """
   124	    Build an ordered list of URLs to try:
   125	    1. Wikipedia direct probes (most reliable)
   126	    2. DDG results for broad query (no quotes)
   127	    3. DDG results for Wikipedia-targeted query
   128	    """
   129	    direct = _try_wikipedia_direct(team_name)
   130	
   131	    # Broad query — no strict quotes so DDG finds more
   132	    broad = _ddg_search(f"{team_name} Nigeria women football NWFL")
   133	    # Wikipedia-targeted fallback
   134	    wiki = _ddg_search(f"{team_name} wikipedia Nigeria football club")
   135	
   136	    ranked = _rank_urls(broad + wiki)
   137	
   138	    # Merge: direct Wikipedia URLs first, then ranked search results
   139	    seen: set[str] = set()
   140	    result: list[str] = []
   141	    for u in direct + ranked:
   142	        if u not in seen:
   143	            seen.add(u)
   144	            result.append(u)
   145	    return result
   146	
   147	
   148	# ── Page extraction ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   149	
   150	def _extract_text(html: str, max_chars: int = 8000) -> str:
   151	    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser")
   152	    for tag in soup(["script", "style", "nav", "footer", "header",
   153	                     "aside", "noscript", "svg", "form"]):
   154	        tag.decompose()
   155	    article = (
   156	        soup.find("div", {"id": "mw-content-text"})
   157	        or soup.find("main")
   158	        or soup.body
   159	    )
   160	    text = (article or soup).get_text(separator="\n", strip=True)
   161	    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
   162	    return text[:max_chars]
   163	
   164	
   165	def _scrape_page(url: str, tmp_dir: str) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
   166	    """Scrape with Playwright. Returns (text, screenshot_path|None)."""
   167	    try:
   168	        with sync_playwright() as pw:
   169	            browser = pw.chromium.launch(headless=True)
   170	            ctx = browser.new_context(
   171	                viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 900},
   172	                user_agent=_HEADERS["User-Agent"],
   173	            )
   174	            page = ctx.new_page()
   175	            try:
   176	                page.goto(url, wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=20_000)
   177	                page.wait_for_timeout(1200)
   178	            except PWTimeout:
   179	                pass
   180	
   181	            html = page.content()
   182	            text = _extract_text(html)
   183	            screenshot_path: str | None = None
   184	            if text:
   185	                sshot = Path(tmp_dir) / "page.png"
   186	                try:
   187	                    page.screenshot(path=str(sshot), full_page=False)
   188	                    screenshot_path = str(sshot)
   189	                except Exception:
   190	                    pass
   191	            ctx.close()
   192	            browser.close()
   193	            return text, screenshot_path
   194	    except Exception as exc:
   195	        return f"[Playwright error: {exc}]", None
   196	
   197	
   198	def _scrape_page_requests(url: str) -> str:
   199	    try:
   200	        resp = requests.get(url, headers=_HEADERS, timeout=12)
   201	        resp.raise_for_status()
   202	        return _extract_text(resp.text)
   203	    except Exception:
   204	        return ""
   205	
   206	
   207	# ── Result quality check ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   208	
   209	def _is_usable(result: dict) -> bool:
   210	    """Accept only results where the AI actually identified the team."""
   211	    if not result:
   212	        return False
   213	    name = result.get("name")
   214	    confidence = result.get("confidence", "LOW")
   215	    # Must have at least a name; LOW confidence with no name = AI found nothing
   216	    if not name:
   217	        return False
   218	    if confidence == "LOW" and not result.get("city") and not result.get("state"):
   219	        return False
   220	    return True
   221	
   222	
   223	def _best_match(results: list[dict], target: str) -> dict | None:
   224	    target_lower = target.lower()
   225	    # Prefer exact or partial name match
   226	    for r in results:
   227	        name = (r.get("name") or "").lower()
   228	        if name and (target_lower in name or name in target_lower):
   229	            if _is_usable(r):
   230	                return r
   231	    # Fall back to first usable result
   232	    for r in results:
   233	        if _is_usable(r):
   234	            return r
   235	    return None
   236	
   237	
   238	# ── Public entry point ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   239	
   240	def research_team(team_name: str) -> dict:
   241	    """
   242	    Research a team by name. Returns a ParsedTeamResult dict.
   243	    Raises ValueError if no usable data found after exhausting all sources.
   244	    """
   245	    candidates = _candidate_urls(team_name)
   246	    if not candidates:
   247	        raise ValueError(f"No candidate URLs found for '{team_name}'.")
   248	
   249	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_dir:
   250	        last_error: Exception | None = None
   251	        tried = 0
   252

Pushed. Here's what the commit does:

### 1. AI Timeout Fix (`ai_parser.py`)
Added `timeout=25.0` to the Anthropic client. No more 30s+ hangs — if OpenRouter free models are slow, the request fails fast and falls back to research.

### 2. Auto-Research Fallback (`teams/views.py`)
`parse_team_ai` now does this:
1. Runs the initial AI parse from text/image
2. Checks if **critical fields are missing** (`city`, `state`, `manager`, `founded`, `bio`) or confidence is `LOW`
3. If so, automatically calls `research_team(name)` — the same web scraper that searches Wikipedia, DDG, etc.
4. Merges the research result: **keeps original non-empty values, fills gaps from web data**
5. Returns the enriched result

So if you type "Pelican Stars FC" and the AI only gives you name + slug, the endpoint will now **automatically scrape the web** to find city, state, manager, etc.

### 3. Prompt Update (`team_parser.md`)
Added a **CRITICAL RULE** telling the AI:
> *"Do NOT return null for fields you can reasonably infer from your training data. If the team is a known NWFL club, fill city and state from your knowledge even if the input does not mention them."*

A simpler approach: try parsing progressively shorter strings by truncating at the last `}` or `]`. Or use regex to extract JSON.

Actually, the cleanest approach is to use regex to find either a JSON object or array:

```python
import re

def _extract_json(text: str) -> str:
    # Try to find a JSON object
    obj_match = re.search(r'\{.*\}', text, re.DOTALL)
    arr_match = re.search(r'\[.*\]', text, re.DOTALL)
    
    # Return whichever appears first and is valid
    candidates = []
    if obj_match:
        candidates.append(obj_match.group(0))
    if arr_match:
        candidates.append(arr_match.group(0))
    
    for candidate in candidates:
        try:
            json.loads(candidate)
            return candidate
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
    
    return text  # fallback to original
```

Wait, but `re.search(r'\{.*\}', text, re.DOTALL)` would match from the first `{` to the LAST `}` in the text, which might include multiple objects or trailing text. That's actually what we want for a single object, but if there's trailing text after the object, the `.*` would be greedy and match everything including the trailing text until the very last `}`.

A better approach: use a JSON-aware extraction or progressively try shorter substrings.

Actually, let me think about this differently. The AI typically returns:
1. Just the JSON object
2. JSON object followed by explanation
3. Markdown fences around JSON
4. JSON array

The issue is "extra data after line 14". This means the JSON is valid up to a certain point, then there's more text.

A robust approach: find the first `{` or `[`, then try to parse by progressively finding matching closing brackets. But that's complex.

A simpler hack: since the JSON is usually a single object or array, I can:
1. Find the first `{` or `[`
2. Try to parse substrings ending at each `}` or `]` position
3. Return the first valid parse

```python
def _extract_json(text: str) -> str:
    text = text.strip()
    # Strip markdown fences first
    if text.startswith("```"):
        lines = text.splitlines()
        text = "\n".join(lines[1:-1]).strip()
    
    # Find first JSON start
    start_obj = text.find('{')
    start_arr = text.find('[')
    
    if start_obj == -1 and start_arr == -1:
        return text
    
    # Prefer whichever starts first
    if start_arr == -1 or (start_obj != -1 and start_obj < start_arr):
        start = start_obj
        end_char = '}'
    else:
        start = start_arr
        end_char = ']'
    
    # Try progressively shorter substrings from the end
    # This handles trailing text after the JSON
    search_text = text[start:]
    pos = len(search_text)
    while pos > 0:
        end = search_text.rfind(end_char, 0, pos)
        if end == -1:
            break
        candidate = search_text[:end + 1]
        try:
            json.loads(candidate)
            return candidate
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            pos = end
            continue
    
    return text
```

This is robust. It finds the first `{` or `[`, then tries to find the matching closing bracket by searching from the end backwards. If it finds a valid JSON parse, it returns just that substring.

**What that error was:** The AI returned valid JSON, then appended an explanation after it — something like:

```json
{
  "name": "Pelican Stars",
  ...
}

```

`json.loads()` choked on the trailing text because it expected **only** JSON. The error says "Extra data: line 14 column 1" — meaning character 229 is where the garbage starts.

**Fix applied:**
- New `_extract_json()` function scans the response, finds the first `{` or `[`, then works backwards from the end to find the matching `}` or `]`. It returns **only** the JSON part, discarding any trailing explanation.
- Prompt now explicitly says: *"Do not add any text before or after the JSON. The response must be pure JSON only."*

After deploy (~1 min), Pelican Stars should parse cleanly even if the LLM is chatty.
