Falls back to web research if AI is unavailable.',\n    request={'multipart/form-data': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'text': {'type': 'string'}, 'image': {'type': 'string', 'format': 'binary'}}}},\n    responses={200: OpenApiTypes.OBJECT, 503: OpenApiTypes.OBJECT},\n)\n@api_view(['POST'])\n@permission_classes([IsAdminUser])\n@parser_classes([MultiPartParser, FormParser, JSONParser])\ndef parse_team_ai(request):\n    text = request.data.get('text', '').strip() or None\n    image = request.FILES.get('image')\n\n    if not text and not image:\n        return Response({'error': 'Provide text or image.'}, status=400)\n\n    image_path = None\n    tmp = None\n    try:\n        if image:\n            import tempfile, os\n            suffix = os.path.splitext(image.name)[1] or '.jpg'\n            tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False, suffix=suffix)\n            for chunk in image.chunks():\n                tmp.write(chunk)\n            tmp.close()\n            image_path = tmp.name\n\n        result = None\n        ai_error = None\n\n        # ── Attempt 1: AI parse (fast, 12s timeout) ──────────────────────────\n        try:\n            result = parse_team(text=text, image_path=image_path)\n        except VisionUnavailableError:\n            ai_error = 'vision_unavailable'\n        except Exception as exc:\n            ai_error = str(exc)\n\n        # AI completely failed — use research result directly\n                        result = [enriched]\n                    else:\n                        # Merge into existing sparse parse\n                        for r in result:\n                            for key in ['city', 'state', 'manager', 'founded', 'titles', 'bio', 'group', 'short_name', 'slug']:\n                                if not r.get(key) and enriched.get(key):\n                                    r[key] = enriched[key]\n                            if enriched.get('confidence') in ('HIGH', 'MEDIUM'):\n                                r['confidence'] = enriched['confidence']\n            except Exception as research_err:\n                # If research also failed and AI failed, bubble up the AI error\n                if result is None:\n                    if ai_error == 'vision_unavailable':\n                        return Response({'error': 'vision_unavailable'}, status=503)\n                    return Response({'error': f'AI failed: {ai_error}. Research also failed: {research_err}'}, status=500)\n\n        if result is None:\n            if ai_error == 'vision_unavailable':\n                return Response({'error': 'vision_unavailable'}, status=503)\n            return Response({'error': ai_error or 'Could not parse team.'}, status=500)\n\n        return Response(result)\n    finally:\n        if tmp:\n            import os\n            try:\n                os.unlink(tmp.name)\n            except OSError:\n                pass\n\n\n@extend_schema(\n    summary='Research teams',\n    description='Research unknown teams via web scraping.',\n    request={'application/json': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'teams': {'type': 'array', 'items': {'type': 'string'}}}}},\n    responses={200: OpenApiTypes.OBJECT, 503: OpenApiTypes.OBJECT},\n)\n@api_view(['POST'])\n@permission_classes([IsAdminUser])\ndef research_teams(request):\n    \"\"\"\n    Given a list of team names not in the DB, research each one via Playwright\n    and return ParsedTeamResult dicts ready for review/save.\n\n    Body: { \"teams\": [\"Adamawa Queens FC\", \"Ekiti Queens FC\"] }\n    Returns: [{ \"team_name\": \"...\", \"result\": {...} | null, \"error\": null | \"...\" }]\n    \"\"\"\n    names = request.data.get('teams', [])\n    if not isinstance(names, list) or not names:\n        return Response({'error': 'teams must be a non-empty list.'}, status=400)\n\n    output = []\n    for name in names:\n        try:\n            result = research_team(name)\n            output.append({'team_name': name, 'result': result, 'error': None})\n        except Exception as exc:\n            err_str = str(exc)\n            # Bubble up account-level quota exhaustion immediately — no point continuing\n            if 'free-models-per-day' in err_str or 'Add 10 credits' in err_str or 'daily free limit' in err_str:\n                return Response({'error': 'ai_quota_exceeded', 'detail': err_str}, status=503)\n            output.append({'team_name': name, 'result': None, 'error': err_str})\n\n    return Response(output)\n"

Pushed. Here's exactly what changed and why it will work now:

---

### Before (broken)
```
User clicks "Parse with AI"
  → AI call starts (25s timeout)
    → AI fails/times out at 25s
      → Gunicorn kills worker at 30s
        → "Failed to fetch" (connection dropped)
```

The research fallback **never ran** because it was inside the same try block as the AI call. When the AI threw, everything died.

### After (fixed)
```
User clicks "Parse with AI"
  → AI call starts (12s timeout)
    → If AI succeeds but sparse → merge research into result
    → If AI fails → immediately try research_team()
      → Research scrapes Wikipedia/DDG (up to ~20s)
        → Returns enriched data
  → Gunicorn timeout is now 60s (plenty of headroom)
```

---

### What will happen for "Pelican Stars FC"

**Scenario A — AI works:** Returns basic data, research fills the gaps (city, state, etc.)

**Scenario B — AI is down/rate-limited:** The 12s timeout fires, the view catches it, immediately calls `research_team("Pelican Stars FC")`, which searches DuckDuckGo + Wikipedia and returns structured data.

**Scenario C — BOTH fail:** You get an actual error message explaining what happened, not "Failed to fetch".

---

### After deploy (~1-2 min)

The `nwfl-api.service` systemd file will be updated with `--timeout 60`, so the deploy workflow will restart Gunicorn with the new limit. Try parsing again then.
